   100	## Implementation Basis
   101	
   102	- `mcp/src/index.js` · `mcp/src/tool-inventory.mjs`: Boundary that creates both
   103	  `tools/list` and mode-specific initialize inventory from the active tool registry.
   104	- `mcp/src/analyze.mjs` · `mcp/src/rust-feature-evidence.mjs` ·
   105	  `mcp/src/infer-imports.mjs`: Bounded repository meaning ingress, Rust configuration provenance,
   106	  evidence for TS/JS/Python file-imports and Go package-imports without execution, Autotools C/Rust unsupported
   107	  scope and exact-path relationship evidence.
   108	- `mcp/src/vault.mjs` · `mcp/src/schema.mjs`: File read/write and UID/slug specifications.
   109	- `mcp/src/ontology-compiler.mjs` · `mcp/src/ontology-engine.mjs`: Compile/query.
   110	- `mcp/src/competency-coverage.mjs` · `mcp/src/meaning-evaluation.mjs`: Quantified
   111	  competency coverage and source-backed proposal write gate.
   112	- `mcp/src/construction-qualification.mjs` · `mcp/src/construction-lifecycle.mjs`:
   113	  Maker-independent categorical qualification, exact plan/source/approval binding, step-by-step
   114	  write eligibility.
   115	- `mcp/src/project-source-inspection.mjs` · `mcp/src/project-source-receipt.mjs`:
   116	  Re-verification of bounded source currentness for installed apps and public receipt boundary.
   117	- `mcp/src/meaning-repair.mjs` · `mcp/src/project-meaning-inventory.mjs`: Current declarations,
   118	  separating structure/source candidates and unresolved targets into action-first human approval packets.
   119	- `mcp/scripts/verify.mjs` · `mcp/src/integration.test.mjs` ·
   120	  `scripts/dogfood-mcp-walk.mjs`: Initialize/tools-list exact parity and installation/live usage verification.
   121	- `mcp/README.md`: Detailed single source of truth for the current public tool contract.

  5883	impact 관계가 아니다”는 유지한다. 그 기록의 세 반증 조건 중 실제 production Go
  5884	import witness를 재현하지 못한 조건은 관측됐지만, scan 시간 초과와 source-hidden
  5885	evidence의 approved impact 오인은 관측되지 않았다. 2026-08-16 C coverage 결정의
  5886	“공개 enum 확장은 계약 변경으로 기록하고 strict consumer를 함께 맞춘다”를 적용한다.
  5887	
  5888	**갈린 지점**: 기존 `edges`의 `to`를 언어별로 file/package로 넓힐지, Go package
  5889	import를 별도 typed receipt로 분리할지 갈렸다. 임의 대표 파일은 전원이 기각했다.
  5890	
  5891	**적용한 규칙**: 최소 슬라이스 규칙 — 기존 JS/TS/Python file edge 의미는 그대로
  5892	두고, Go에 필요한 package endpoint만 versioned branch로 추가한다.
  5893	
  5894	**권고 (chief)**: README는 기존 1,200자·8 heading·6문서·256 KiB 상한 안에서
  5895	purpose·responsibility·ability block을 결정론적으로 고르고 sponsor/backer/TOC
  5896	section을 제외한다. 공개 `infer_imports`는 root `go.mod`의 module-local import만
  5897	bounded text로 읽어 `{fromFile,toPackage,...}` typed evidence를 반환하고, analyzer와
  5898	`index_project`가 같은 결과를 재사용한다. 기존 file edges, 5,000-file 상한,
  5899	candidate/source-hidden qualification, no-write/사람 승인 경계는 유지한다.
  5900	**서명 (accountable: 소유자)**: 전체 작업 진행과 검증 후 main 병합 요청에 따라 집행
  5901	
  5902	**기록된 반대**: 별도 package branch는 schema·CLI·bundle 소비자 비용이 크므로,
  5903	같은 `edges.to`에 root-contained package directory를 넣고 문구만 source dependency
  5904	endpoint로 넓히는 편이 더 작다. — **반증 조건**: 새 package receipt가 README-only
  5905	보강보다 held-out q4/q5를 유의미하게 높이지 못하거나, 4일 appetite/source·bundle
  5906	parity를 넘기거나, 직접 MCP 소비자가 새 branch를 안전하게 이어받지 못하면 이 반대가
  5907	옳다. — **재검토**: 동일 held-out q1~q6와 direct `infer_imports`/`index_project`,
  5908	defect-reversion, strict consumer parity 측정 직후
  5909	
  5910	**슬라이스**: IN semantic README block selection · root Go module-local package imports ·
  5911	typed public package receipt · analyzer/index 재사용 · bounded package elements · MCP/CLI/docs/
  5912	ontology/source-bundle parity · held-out 재시험 · OUT `go list`/compiler/network · AST/symbol/
  5913	call graph · nested modules/workspaces · arbitrary representative file · capability/depends_on
  5914	자동 승격 · writer/UI/score/qualification 우회 · appetite 최대 4일
  5915	
  5916	**집행 측정**: 낯선 Apache-licensed 대형 Go 저장소에서 direct source scan은 2,725개
  5917	Go file·6,358개 module-local import·1,696개 package boundary를 읽었고 세 번 중앙값은
  5918	1.784초였다. 실제 stdio MCP candidate gate는 제한 2,000 file에서 4,330 import·1,307
  5919	package boundary를 전달하고 legacy file edge 0, 인용 경로 9/9, vault write 0을 보였다.
  5920	source-hidden evaluator는 full body 8/8·relation 7/7·digest/revision/gap order와 세 변조
  5921	거부를 확인했다. q1~q5는 `weak`, q6는 누락 목록으로 완전 답변됐지만 underlying evidence는
  5922	`missing`이었다. 첫 packet에서 발견된 scope-promotion 위험 때문에 epistemic unknown을
  5923	`Excludes`에서 `Uncertainty`/gap으로 이동시키고 `epistemic-exclusion-boundary` validator를
  5924	추가했다. 수정 packet은 proposal validation 0 error·5 honest warning, source digest 동일,
  5925	새 plan digest, `not_qualified`·write 0을 유지했다. 이는 transport/분석 개선이지 완성된
  5926	business ontology 자격 판정이 아니다.
  5927	
  5928	**상태**: 유효

 11190	User moment 3 · Differentiation 3 · Ontology value 4 · Agent value 4 ·
 11191	Verification 0, 치명적 0: Verification). 실제 restart proof 전에는 공개 등록을
 11192	승인하지 않는다.

 11194	**선행 결정 관계**: 바로 아래 「의미 평가는 구조 readiness와 분리한 순수 계약부터
 11195	연다」는 유효하다. 그 기록이 OUT으로 둔 sidecar persistence와 public MCP field를
 11196	이번에 한꺼번에 여는 것이 아니라, 저장된 ontology에서 typed competency를 다시 읽는
 11197	최소 commit point부터 증명한다. 「프로젝트의 현재 source 근거는 하나의 receipt로
 11198	사람과 agent에게 보인다」의 0/1 source cardinality·private root 비노출·현재성 분리도
 11199	그대로 유지한다.
 11200	
 11201	**관측**: `meaningAssessment:v1`은 순수 함수이고 현재 `agent_brief`는 source receipt만
 11202	결합한다. `buildWritePlan`은 승인된 다섯 typed competency answer를 사람이 읽는
 11203	project Markdown의 `## Competency answers`로 이미 보존하지만, version/evaluator·
 11204	graph hash·source fingerprint를 묶어 새 MCP 프로세스가 다시 검증하는 reader가 없다.
 11205	dogfood project 본문에도 아직 그 section이 없어서 구조 `ready 100/100`과 source
 11206	53/53만으로는 의미 질문의 현재 답·gap을 복원할 수 없다. 반복 재분석 행동 자체는
 11207	아직 관측되지 않았으므로 사람 UI 필요성이나 숫자 분석률로 확대하지 않는다.
 11208	
 11209	**결정 (accountable: 소유자)**: 첫 commit point는 project Markdown을 답·typed
 11210	witness·gap의 유일한 사람이 편집 가능한 정본으로 유지한다. 모든 concept/relation
 11211	write와 `validate_vault`·project compile이 성공한 뒤에만 내부 finalize가 evaluator
 11212	version·project body digest·현재 graph hash·source fingerprint·measuredAt을 local
 11213	sidecar receipt로 원자 기록한다. sidecar에는 raw answer, raw body, overall status,
 11214	absolute root, private remote를 복제하지 않는다. read-back은 Markdown과 receipt 및
 11215	현재 graph/source inventory를 다시 대조해 `meaningAssessment:v1`을 매번 파생하며,
 11216	legacy/missing·malformed·digest mismatch·ghost witness·graph/source drift를 절대
 11217	`verified_current`로 승격하지 않는다.
 11218	
 11219	**공개 등록 게이트**: 먼저 internal helper와 synthetic temporary vault로
 11220	write → validate → compile → finalize → MCP 종료 → 새 MCP process `agent_brief`
 11221	read-back을 RED→GREEN으로 증명한다. 이어 body 외부 편집, relation/witness 삭제,
 11222	future/malformed receipt, source-hidden, private path leak, finalize 실패 주입에서
 11223	fail-closed와 부분 commit 0을 증명한다. 이 증거로 결 자리의 Verification이 4가 되기
 11224	전에는 새 MCP tool과 `agent_brief.meaningAssessment`를 registry에 넣지 않는다.
 11225	
 11226	**적용 규칙**: 최대 2일 · 합집합 금지. IN — versioned Markdown serializer/parser,
 11227	post-write finalize core, body digest·graph/source provenance receipt, current inventory
 11228	재검증, fresh-process integration proof, proof 뒤 전용 finalize MCP tool 1개와 기존
 11229	`agent_brief`의 categorical field 1개. OUT — 지도/UI, percentage·combined confidence,
 11230	새 CLI command, `analyze_repo_structure`의 side effect, watcher, multi-root, 범용
 11231	AST/source-role scanner, Ollama routing, 외부 field-trial 결과물 반입, legacy 자동 migration.
 11232	
 11233	**기록된 반대**: versioned Markdown parser만으로도 다음 세션이 답과 witness를 복원할
 11234	수 있는데 별도 finalize receipt를 요구하면 사람이 Markdown을 고친 뒤 stale 상태와
 11235	추가 마찰만 만든다.
 11236	**반증 조건**: 두 독립 MCP 프로세스가 receipt 없이 project Markdown만으로 같은
 11237	typed CQ와 current graph/source provenance를 손실 없이 복원하고, 부분 graph write나
 11238	동시 외부 편집 뒤에도 false current를 만들지 않는다면 finalize 계약은 불필요하다.
 11239	**재검토**: fresh-process proof에서 finalize가 없을 때도 위 조건이 성립하거나,
 11240	finalize 누락이 정상 handoff를 반복적으로 막는 첫 관측 시.
 11241	
 11242	**상태**: 유효.

    78	
    79	In Python cold start, read `README.rst`, non-executed static `setup.py` package contract, and top-level `__init__.py` package as bounded evidence. `infer_imports` condenses static imports from that package into file/module edges. This result is implementation evidence; do not auto-promote to domain/capability/semantic `depends_on`. Each module edge carries up to 5 exact file-edge receipts and presence of other evidence. Edges not in the vault return `rationale_review_required` instead of executable `proposedAction`. If the implementation path is already known, use `focusPath` or `reviewMode:"focus"` first. This mode returns exact incoming/outgoing static import counts (up to 100 receipts) and a stateless cursor for that file without a loadable vault. Empty results do not imply no impact; they fix symbol/test/dynamic behavior and ontology meaning as still pending separate confirmation.     89	## Constraints: Impact and blast radius
    90	
    91	`impact` and `blast_radius` follow only declared `depends_on`. Containment/domain/element relations are structural evidence for `reachability`/`subgraph`; do not promote to impact or risk. Return `review_required` if no `relation_notes` on declared dependency, `declared_with_rationale` if present. Both are currently not source-backed due to lack of current-source receipt per relation unit; keep completeness and risk as `unknown`. Therefore, 0 dependency declarations are not interpreted as low-risk or no impact.
    92	
    93	## Inclusions / Exclusions
    94	
    95	- Included: MCP tool registration and I/O contracts, Vault parser/writer, deterministic compiler and
    96	  graph query, concurrency/dry-run/validation safeguards, bundled server for installed apps.
    97	- Excluded: AST/source search engine, embedding store, model selection/agent loop, backend/accounts,
    98	  auto-saving creation proposals without human approval.
    99	
   100	## Implementation Basis
    27	The minimum supported path is intentionally plain: connect Atlas MCP or run the
    28	Atlas CLI from Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, or another coding agent, and the agent
    29	should already receive a useful workspace brief, graph health check, handoff
    30	packet, and reviewable memory-diff workflow. No CodeGraph, Serena, language
    31	server, grep wrapper, or external source-index service is required for the
    32	product to be usable.
    33	
    34	Atlas should not promise to replace code-reading tools. Built-in source search,
    35	grep, language servers, Serena, CodeGraph, AST indexes, and similar tools are
    36	good at structural facts: definitions, callers, imports, routes, and local
    37	impact paths. Atlas helps the coding agent one layer above that: it preserves
    38	the task starting point, product meaning, capability boundary, implementation
    39	evidence, and validation path that explain why those code facts matter.
    40	
    41	## Product Bet
    42	
    43	The product is not valuable because it is "an ontology editor." Developers do
    44	not want another system they must manually maintain.
    45	
    46	The product is valuable if it reduces the maintenance cost of AI-agent memory:
    47	
    48	1. Open a repo and generate an ontology draft automatically.
    49	2. Let the agent propose mental-model changes after real code work.
    50	3. Let the developer review those changes like a git diff.
    51	4. Make the next agent session visibly better because the memory is already in
    52	   the repo.
    53	
    54	That loop is the product. The ontology graph is the implementation substrate.
    55	
    56	The coding-agent value is therefore not "Atlas reads all code for you." The
    57	value is "Atlas tells the agent what to inspect, why it matters, and what must
    58	be verified before acting." Store meaningful implementation evidence as
    59	`element` nodes and relations; leave exhaustive symbol graphs to deterministic
    60	code-intelligence tools.
    61	
   199	
   200	When explaining the code side, use this boundary:
   201	
   202	```text
   203	Built-in search, grep, language servers, Serena, CodeGraph, and source indexes
   204	find code structure.
   205	Ontology Atlas preserves product/system meaning, implementation evidence, and
   206	the next verification path for humans and AI agents.
   207	```
    93	AST/code-index and source-intelligence tools such as language servers, Serena,
    94	CodeGraph, and built-in search are reference points for the
    95	implementation-evidence side of that loop: parse deterministically, index
    96	locally, answer structural questions without re-reading the repo. Atlas should
    97	learn from those properties without copying implementation: local-first
    98	indexing, stable node identifiers, incremental refresh, query-plan style
    99	narrowing, and impact traversal. Atlas' distinct value is the curated business
   100	and product meaning layer on top: what the organization is trying to do, which
   101	capabilities carry it, which implementation proves it, what changes affect it,
   102	and what a human or AI agent should verify before acting.
   103	
   104	The coding-agent promise is not that Atlas replaces source search or stores an
   105	exhaustive symbol graph. Built-in search, grep, language servers, Serena,
   106	CodeGraph, and AST indexes should remain optional structural layers for
   107	definitions, callers, imports, and local code impact. Atlas must work for plain
   108	Claude Code or Codex after only the Atlas MCP server or CLI is connected; no
   109	external code index, source-intelligence service, or extra agent plugin may be a
   110	precondition for first value. Source tools are accelerators after the Atlas
   111	meaning packet has narrowed the work. Atlas should tell the agent which
   112	structural question to ask, which capability or domain gives that code meaning,
   113	and which verification path makes the answer safe to use. The ontology should
   114	therefore store meaningful implementation evidence, not every code fact: files,
   115	classes, commands, routes, tests, and MCP tools become `element` nodes only when
   116	they help trace a business/product capability, impact path, or agent handoff.

codex
기록상 `mcp-server` capability의 **범위 밖**입니다.

- 현재 `docs/ontology/capabilities/mcp-server.md`의 `Inclusions / Exclusions`는 MCP 도구 등록·I/O, Vault parser/writer, ontology graph query는 포함하지만, **`AST/source search engine`은 명시적으로 제외**합니다.
- 기존 `focusPath`도 파일 단위 static import 증거까지만 다루며, `symbol coupling`, affected behavior, runtime impact는 주장하지 않는다고 기록돼 있습니다. [DECISIONS.md](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/DECISIONS.md:7051)
- 그 범위가 부족할 때의 다음 선택도 `mcp-server` 확장이 아니라 **별도의 `symbol/CodeGraph integration boundary` 설계**로 기록돼 있습니다. [DECISIONS.md](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/DECISIONS.md:7064)
- 제품 경계 역시 definitions, callers, imports 같은 구조 검색은 CodeGraph·AST·source-search 계층에 두고, Atlas는 의미·승인·구현 증거·검증 경로를 담당한다고 명시합니다. [PRODUCT-DIRECTION.md](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/PRODUCT-DIRECTION.md:104), [AGENT-MEMORY-POSITIONING.md](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/AGENT-MEMORY-POSITIONING.md:34)

따라서 기록된 경계는 다음과 같습니다: **MCP transport와 tool registry는 `mcp-server` 안, 코드 심볼 검색 엔진의 책임은 별도 경계 밖**입니다. 다만 그 별도 capability의 이름이나 관계는 아직 확정 기록이 없고, “별도 symbol/CodeGraph integration boundary를 설계한다”까지만 기록돼 있습니다.
